{D2627}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_sarba durga ti pa ri sho d+ha ne te dzo rA dza kalpa A lo kA laM ka ra nA ma/_bod skad du/_ngan song 
[\[219b.7\]]thams cad yongs su sbyong ba gzi brjid kyi rgyal po brtag pa snang ba'i rgyan zhes bya ba/_gtso chen kun rig shAkya thub/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa zhi dang khro/_/tshe dpag med dang bde ba che/_/'khor dang bcas la phyag 'tshal nas/_/ngan song rnam par sbyong byed pa'i/_/gzi brjid 
[\[220a\]]
[\[220a.1\]]rgyal po'i brtag pa ni/_bdag nyid dran pa skyed pa'i phyir/_/ci nus pas ni rnam par dbye/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes bya ba la sogs pas 'di'i brjod par bya ba dang 'brel pa ni gang zhig yin/_dgos pa dang dgos pa'i dgos pa ni ci zhig 
[\[220a.2\]]yod ce na/_de brjod par bya ste/_ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba gzi brjid kyi rgyal po'i brtag pa zhes bya ba ni rgyud yin la/_'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes bya ba nas tshangs pa chen po pad+ma'i gdan la bzhugs te zhes bya ba'i bar gyis shAkya thub pa nyid dang gzhan gyi don 
[\[220a.3\]]phun sum tshogs pa'i rang bzhin ston pa'i gleng gzhi'i mdo ni/_gdul bya mngon par 'dod pa bskyed pa'i phyir gsungs pa yin no/_/gdul bya mngon par 'dod pa skyes pa rnams la shAkya thub pa de mngon sum du byed pa'i thabs ni ngan song gi rgyud thams cad yongs su sbyong ba'i ting 
[\[220a.4\]]nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te zhes bya ba nas brtsams te/_bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do zhes bya ba'i bar gyi gzhung gis thabs bstan to/_/yang 'di kun rig dang gtsug tor dgu dang /_tshe dpag tu med pa'i dkyil 'khor ni bar ma la srid pa 
[\[220a.5\]]rjes su 'dzin par byed pa yin no/_/bde ba chen po dang /_'chi bdag 'joms pa dang /_khro bo rdo rje me ni/_mchog la srid pa rjes su 'dzin par byed pa yin no/_/'jig rten pa'i dkyil 'khor rnams ni mchog dang bar ma ma yin pa la srid pa rjes su 'dzin par byed pa yin no/_/
[\[220a.6\]]ji skad du 'di nyid las/_srid dang srid min rnam sbyong ba/_/zhi zhing dul la dga' ba che/_/zhes gsungs pa'i phyir ro/_/yang 'dir ni kun rig dang bde ba chen po'i dkyil 'khor ni/_sku ston zhing phyag rgya chen po yin la/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su bstan te/_
[\[220a.7\]]'dod chags kyis 'dod chags spang ba'i phyir gsungs pa yin no/_/gtsug tor dang 'chi bdag 'joms pa'i dkyil 'khor ni gsung ston zhing chos kyi phyag rgya gtso bor gyur la/_pad+ma'i rigs su bstan te gti mug gis gti mug spang ba'i phyir gsungs pa yin no/_/tshe 
[\[220b\]]
[\[220b.1\]]dpag med dang khro bo nyi ma dang me dang 'jig rten pa'i dkyil 'khor rnams ni thugs ston zhing dam tshig gi phyag rgya gtso bor gyur la rdo rje'i rigs yin te/_khro bas khro ba spang ba'i phyir gsungs pa yin no/_/ji skad du 'di nyid las/_lag pa g.yon pa'i thal mo bkan te pang du 
[\[220b.2\]]bzhag la de'i steng du g.yas pa bzhag ste mthe bong gnyis sprad la zhi ba'i lta bas blta ba 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi phyag rgya ste dam tshig yin par bshad do/_/de nyid las bzlog ste ltag 'og bsnol la mthe bong dang mthe'u chung gnyis gcig 
[\[220b.3\]]la gcig lu gu rgyud kyi tshul du bsnol la snying gar gzhag pa 'di ni rdo rje rigs kyi dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/thal mo rdzogs par sbyar ba las mthe bong mthe'u chung gnyis bsnyams pa la lhag ma brkyang ba 'di ni pad+mo'i rigs kyi pad+mo'i phyag rgya'o zhes bya ba dang /_rtse gsum 
[\[220b.4\]]rtse gsum 'chang 'phro dang /_/'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug gtso/_/zhes pa dang /_ma rig 'joms pa'i tshangs pa ste/_/'dod chags zhe sdang dri mthar mdzad/_/ces gsungs pa'i phyir ro/_/yang kun rig dang bde ba chen po ni longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku'i tshul/_
[\[220b.5\]]shAkya thub pa gtsug tor gyi dkyil 'khor dang /_'chi bdag 'joms pa sprul pa'i sku'i tshul/_dkyil 'khor lhag ma rnams ni chos kyi sku'i tshul ston pa yin no/_/'di nyid las ji skad du bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de nyid longs spyod rdzogs pa'i skus rdo rje sems 
[\[220b.6\]]dpa'i skur zhugs par 'tshal bar bgyi ste zhes gsungs pa'i phyir ro/_/yang 'di'i dbang dang dam tshig dngos grub kyi dkyil 'khor kun rig dang /_gtsug tor dgu dang /_tshe dpag med dang /_rgyal chen bzhi dang /_phyogs skyong bcu dang /_gza' chen brgyad dang /_klu chen 
[\[220b.7\]]brgyad dang /_'jigs byed chen po dang /_lha chen rnams kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_'chi bdag 'joms pa dang /_bde ba chen po dang /_khro bo rdo rje nyi ma me'i dkyil 'khor te bcu gnyis so/_/bsgrub pa'i dkyil 'khor kun rig gi ras la bri ba dang /_gtsug tor dgu'i 
[\[221a\]]
[\[221a.1\]]ras la bri ba dang /_tshe dpag med kyi ras la bri ba dang /_klu chen brgyad kyi bsam pa dang /_'jigs byed chen po'i bsam pa dang /_lha chen po'i bsam pa dang /_'chi bdag 'joms pa'i bsam pa dang /_bde ba chen po'i bsam pa dang /_rdo rje nyi ma me'i 
[\[221a.2\]]bsam pa ste dgu'o/_de kho na ste dkyil 'khor gyi de kho na nyid do/_/dkyil 'khor bskyed pa'i gsang sngags dang /_byin gyis brlab pa'i gsang sngags dang /_mchod pa'i sngags dang /_dbang dang dam tshig sbyin pa'i sngags dang /_bsgrub pa'i sngags dang /_brdung ba'i sngags 
[\[221a.3\]]dang /_dgug pa'i sngags dang /_sngags kyi de kho na nyid de ci rigs su sbyar ro/_/'di'i kun rig gi skabs na lcags kyu la sogs pa'i phyag rgya dang /_gtsug tor dang bde ba chen po'i skabs nas bshad pa ste phyag rgya'i de kho na nyid do/_/klu chen brgyad dang /_'jigs byed 
[\[221a.4\]]chen po dang /_lha chen po brgyad kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_'chi bdag 'joms pa dang /_bde ba chen po dang /_khro bo rdo rje nyi ma dang /_me'i dkyil 'khor gyi skabs nas bsgom pa'i sgo nas bsgrub pa drug ste ting nge 'dzin gyi de kho na nyid do/_/yang kun rig gi ras ris kyi bzlas pa 
[\[221a.5\]]dang /_ro'am gzugs brnyan la bzlas pa dang /_gtsug tor dgu'i ras ris dang /_ro'am gzugs brnyan la bzlas pa dang /_tshe dpag med kyi ras ris kyi bzlas pa dang /_klu chen brgyad kyi bzlas pa dang /_lha chen dang 'jigs byed chen po'i bzlas pa dang /_'chi bdag 
[\[221a.6\]]'joms pa'i skabs dang /_khro bo'i sbyin sreg gi skabs kyi bzlas pa dang bcu ni bzlas brjod kyi de kho na nyid do/_/yang kun rig gi skabs nas zhi ba'i don du sbyin sreg dang /_ro bsreg pa'i don du zhi ba'i sbyin sreg dang /_gtsug tor dgu'i don du zhi ba'i sbyin sreg dang /_
[\[221a.7\]]'chi bdag 'joms pa'i zhi ba dang /_rgyas pa dang /_dbang dang /_mngon spyod kyi sbyin sreg dang /_bde ba chen po'i skabs nas zhi ba dang /_rgyas pa dang /_dbang dang /_mngon spyod dang /_rdo rje nyi ma me'i zhi ba dang /_rgyas pa dang /_dbang dang /_mngon spyod dang bcu bzhi ste/_sbyin 
[\[221b\]]
[\[221b.1\]]sreg gi de kho na nyid do/_/rdo rje nyi ma me ma gtogs pa'i dkyil 'khor bcu gcig pa la sogs pa nas spyan drang ba bstan pa ni spyan drang ba'i de kho na nyid do/_/dkyil 'khor de dag dang sbyin sreg dang ras ris la sogs pa rnams las mchod pa bshad pa rnams dang /_rdo rje 
[\[221b.2\]]nyi ma me'i sbyin sreg gi skabs nas lha rnams mchod cing gshegs su gsol ba'i de kho na nyid bstan to/_/kun rig gi ras bris kyi skabs nas 'jig rten gsum rgyal du bskyed de bdag gi de nyid dran pas ni bdag bsrung ba ni srung ba'i de kho na nyid bstan to/_/yang dngos su 
[\[221b.3\]]ting nge 'dzin thabs yin pas de brjod par bya ste/_de yang rtsa ba'i rgyud kyis bshad par bya'o/_/ngan song thams cad kyi rgyud yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya ba nas brtsams te sangs rgyas bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa bzhis bshad pa ngas bshad kyis nyon cig ces bya ba'i bar gyis 
[\[221b.4\]]ngan 'gro sbyong zhing nor bu dri ma med pa lung bstan pa ste dang po sbyor ba'i ting nge 'dzin to/_/de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin gyis zhes bya ba nas brtsams te/_'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i phan pa dang bde ba thams cad myong bar 'gyur ro zhes bya 
[\[221b.5\]]ba'i bar gyi gzhung gis tshangs pa la sogs pa'i 'jig rten pa'i lha'i dkyil 'khor du dgod pa'i phyir dang lha rnams bskyed pa'i phyir gsang sngags bshad pa ste dkyil 'khor rgyal po mchog gi ting nge 'dzin ston to/_/de nas bsdu zhing byin gyis brlab pa nas brtsams 
[\[221b.6\]]te las kyi rgyal po mchog 'phags pa gsang zhing sra ba'i mchog mthon po las bshad pa bzhin du shes par bya'o/_/dbang dang dam tshig thob pas thabs 'di nyid la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po zhes bya ba nas brtsams te/_brtser ldan dngos grub myur 
[\[221b.7\]]du 'grub/_/ces bya ba'i bar gyi gzhung gis slob dpon gyi las kyi cho ga dang /_slob ma gzhug pa la sogs pa'i cho ga ston to/_/de nyid du dbang dang dam tshig thob pa rnams la/_ji ltar 'dod pa'i dngos grub bslab par bya'o zhes bya ba'i don 'di bstan par bya ba'i phyir/_
[\[222a\]]
[\[222a.1\]]de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin zhes bya ba nas/_gnod sbyin rgyu skar gdong la sogs/_/tshig tsam gyis ni dran bzhin du/_/zhi ba la sogs las kun byed/_/ces bya ba'i bar gyi gzhung gis dngos grub ston 
[\[222a.2\]]to/_/yang 'di nyid du/_dbang dang dam tshig thob pa rnams la/_sdig pa chen po byas pas sems can dmyal ba la sogs pa'i nang du gyur pa rnams thabs sla bas grol bar bya ba'i don 'di bstan par bya ba'i phyir/_de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin gyis zhes bya ba 
[\[222a.3\]]nas/_bde gshegs rgyal ba nyid kyis gsungs/_/zhes bya ba'i bar gyi gzhung gis shi ba'i dbang du gyur pa'i cho ga bstan to/_/de nas brgya byin la sogs lhas/_/pad+ma lta bu'i mig phye nas/_/zhes pa nas brtsams te bag yod par gyis la sgrubs shig ces bya ba'i 
[\[222a.4\]]bar gyi gzhung gis nor bu dri ma med pa dga' ldan du skyes pa dang /_rgyud gtad pa bstan te/_rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi bsdus pa'i don bstan to/_/rgyud phyi ma'i bsdus pa'i don bstan par bya ste de las kyang dang por gtsug tor dgu'i bsdus pa'i don bstan pa ste/_'dir dang por 
[\[222a.5\]]sbyor ba'i ting nge 'dzin ni rtsa ba'i rgyud las bshad pa las phyag na rdo rje dang bcas pa'o/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rje zhes bya ba nas brtsams te/_hUM phaT ces pa'i bar gyi gzhung gis dkyil 'khor rgyal mchog bstan to/_/las rgyal 
[\[222a.6\]]mchog ni snga ma bzhin du shes par bya'o/_/thabs de la 'di nyid du dbang dang dam tshig thob pas 'jug par bya'o zhes bya ba'i don 'di bstan par bya ba'i phyir/_de nas dkyil 'khor bshad de zhes bya ba nas brtsams te/_phyag na rdo rje hUM zhes bya ba las thams cad byed 
[\[222a.7\]]pa'i bar gyi gzhung gis slob dpon gyi las dang /_slob ma gzhug pa la sogs pa'i cho ga bstan to/_/'di las gsungs pa'i dngos grub rnams bya ba dang spyod pa dang rnal 'byor gyi rgyud gzhan las gsungs pa'i thabs kyis 'grub bo zhes bya bar brtag pa gzhan gyi cho

[\[222b.5\]]smin pa ci las gyur pa bstan to/_/gtsug tor dgu'i bsdus pa'i don to/_/da ni tshe tshad med pa'i bsdus pa'i don bshad par bya ste/_sbyor ba dang po'i ting nge 'dzin ni gtsug tor du bshad pa nyid do/_/de nas brgya byin dang tshangs pa rnams yid rab tu mgu zhing rangs nas zhes 
[\[222b.6\]]bya ba nas/_gsang ba'i bdag pos bshad du gsol/_/zhes bya ba'i gzhung gis bstod cing gsol ba gdab par ston to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rjes zhes bya ba nas rmad kyi ya mtshan du ma 'jig rten du snang bar gyur to zhes bya ba'i bar gyi gzhung gis

[\[229a.3\]]kyi 'dod chags chen po ste/_gang phyir sems ni bcom ldan 'das/_/'dod chags chen po 'di yi lha/_/zhes gsungs pa'i phyir ro/_/dga' ba'i tshal mchog tu yid du 'ong ba na zhes bya ba ni chos nyid kyis de bzhin du ro gcig pa'o/_/ji skad du/_blo mchog rnams kyi kun dga' ra ba 
[\[229a.4\]]ste/_/dman pas 'jigs pa nyid kyi rgyal byed tshal/_/zhes gsungs pa dang /_chos kyi 'dod pa yid du 'ong /_/'dod chags dam tshig yin par bshad/_/ces gsungs pa'i phyir ro/_/nor bu dang gser shing yal ga dang lcug mar bcas pa dang shing gel pa dang /_sman dang /_me 
[\[229a.5\]]tog ka ma la dang /_karni ka dang /_tsa ku la dang /_ti la ka dang /_a shwa ka dang /_man+dA ra ba dang /_man+dA ra ba chen po la sogs pa sna tshogs pa dang /_dpag bsam gyi shing rnams kyis mdzes par byas pa rgyan sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa zhes bya ba ni/_bcom ldan 'das de nyid sman dang shing 
[\[229a.6\]]dang me tog dang mdzes pa'i rgyan yin te/_'di skad du/_sman rnams ma lus ljon pa'i shing /_/nad do cog gi dgra che ba/_sdu gu 'jig rten gsum na mdzes/_/zhes pa dang /_byang chub yan lag me tog phreng /_/zhes gsungs pa'i phyir ro/_/bya mang po skad 'byin pa zhes pa ni de

[\[229b.3\]]pa'i phyir ro/_/lha dang mi'i bu mo sna tshogs zhes bya ba ni byang chub tu sems bskyed pa dang /_sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la sogs pa ste/_ji skad du/_sngon chad byang chub sems bzang gang /_/rdo rje sems dpar yang dag bshad/_/ces pa dang /_gang phyir mchog tu rol pa na/_/

[\[246a.2\]]gi sdom pa yongs su gzung ba lhag par gnas pa'i cho ga ji lta ba bzhin du byas pas/_slob dpon gyi zhabs gnyis la phyag byas te 'di skad ces brjod par bya'o/_/dga' chen khyod bdag ston pa bas/_/slob dpon khyod ni dgongs su gsol/_/byang chub sems dpa'i tshul bstan pa/_/
[\[246a.3\]]mgon po de ring bdag 'tshal lo/_/dam tshig chen po de nyid gsol/_/sdom pa rnams kyang bdag la gsol/_/de nas yol ba'i mthar rdo rje srung ba yongs su bzlas pa'i ras shin tu dmar bas stod g.yogs smad g.yogs bskon zhing /_sgo srung bzlas pa'i dar dkar gyi gdong g.yogs 
[\[246a.4\]]bcings la phyag byed du gzhug go/_/me tog thogs pas yang slob dpon la phyag byas la/_sdig pa bshags pa dang /_rjes su yi rang ba dang bskul ba dang /_gsol ba gdab pa yang byas la 'di skad ces brjod par bya'o/_/gtso bo bdag la sdom pa gsol/_/thub pa'i nyi ma ma lus 
[\[246a.5\]]pa/_/sangs rgyas bdag la dgongs su gsol/_/bdag ming 'di zhes bya ba ni/_/slob dpon dpang du gnas pa la/_/sangs rgyas rol mo las byung ba/_/mi ldog 'khor lo'i 'byor par ldan/_/thar pa chen po'i grong khyer mchog_/gsang ba chen por 'jug par bgyi/_/gsang ba'i rigs mtho 
[\[246a.6\]]thams cad du/_/slob dpon bdag ni 'jug par mdzod/_/mi ldog pa yi dbang bskur ba/_/skal ba chen po bdag la gsol/_/mtshan rnams kun gyis rab brgyan cing /_/dpe byad rnams dang yang dag ldan/_/sangs rgyas sku ni yid 'ong ba/_/slob dpon chen po bdag la stsol/_/dbang bskur

[\[284a.2\]]bar bsnyen pa dang sgrub par bsams nas nges par brlag par 'gyur ro/_/gzhan yang khro bo 'jig rten gsum snang kha dog dkar po bsgom zhing /_de nas byung ba'i dkyil 'khor gyis khyab par bsams la 'od de phyung nas rims la sogs pa ring du dor la 'od las hUM byung rdo rjer bsam 
[\[284a.3\]]zhing 'od las hUM lta bu'i 'khor lor gyur pa go char bya/_'od zer gyi chus spyi bo nas dbang bskur/_de nas ming nas smos shing bzlas brjod dang bsnyen pa dang bsgrub pa tshul bzhin du bsams na 'grub par 'gyur ba ni/_khro bo 'jig rten gsum snang mdzad/_/zla ba 'dra ba'i dge bsams 
[\[284a.4\]]shing /_/'od zer phyung ba mang po yis/_/khyad par gyur par rnam bsam la/_/rims dang gdon dang dug la sogs/_/'od zer des ni phyung nas kyang /_/de yi nad bgegs gnod pa sems/_/zhi bya'i phyir ni ring du dor/_/de nas hUM gi tshogs bzhag la/_/rdo rje lta bur lus 
[\[284a.5\]]bsam zhing /_/hUM gi 'khor los go char bya/_/'od zer chu yis dbang bskur ro/_/de nas de yi ming smos shing /_/bzlas pa'i tshul gyis bsam par bya/_/zhes gsungs pa yin no/_/sbyin sreg rnam pa gsum bstan pa ni de nas/_khung bu gsum las rnam gcig ni/_/
[\[284a.6\]]zhes bya ba la sogs pas dang po zhi ba bstan te/_de yang zhi ba dang rgyas pa dang dbang gsum las/_zhi ba ni kha dog dkar po/_tha ma ni khru gang /_bar pa khru do/_mchog khru bzhi'o/_/rnal 'byor pa des dang por sa rnams rab sbyangs te/_'khor lo'i steng na zla ba'i dkyil 
[\[284a.7\]]'khor dbus su rdo rje rtse lnga pa cig thab khung gi dbus su bri/_de'i phyi rol gyi 'khor lo rtsibs bcu gnyis pa 'jig rten gsum snang la sogs pa'i phyag rgya 'od zer dkar por 'phro ba/_'dod chags la sogs pa'i rigs brgya 'joms pa bye brag tu phye ba bri/_rgyal po bzhi'i phyag mtshan 
[\[284b\]]
[\[284b.1\]]phyogs skyong rnams kyi phyag mtshan phyogs phyogs su bri/_lha chen po khyab 'jug la sogs pa rnams kyi phyag mtshan dkyil 'khor lta bur bri/_dri zhim pa'i chus bkang ba'i mdog dkar po'i ras kyis dkris pa'i bum pa brgyad gzhag/_'bras chan dang zho dang mar dang dkar gsum 
[\[284b.2\]]gyi gtor ma dang /_lha bshos dang /_bdug pa dang /_mar me dang me tog rnams kyang gzhag_/mchod pa bya ba'i phyir bla re dang /_gdugs dang rgyal mtshan dang rnga ma la sogs pa gzhag/_na ma re re ga pur dang bcas pa'i dri zhim po tsan+dan la sogs pa'i byug pas byugs 
[\[284b.3\]]kha shar du bltas te sbyin sreg bya/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i skyil krung ngam sems dpa'i skyil krung gis gdan la 'dug_/gzhan yang gos la sogs pa dkar po dang /_phreng ba dkar pos brgyan/_sreg pa'i rdzas bstan pa ni mar me dang sbrang rtsi dang /_'o ma dang /_nas kyi thug

[\[284b.5\]]bu yod cing zho dang ldan pa'i yam shing la khung bu'i khyad par gyi sor gyis gzhal zhing /_mchog sor nyi shu rtsa bzhi yan chad/_sor bcu drug thab khung bar ma la/_bcu gnyis dang brgyad tha ma la/_gzhan yang bud shing zho dang ldan pa la dri'i chus bskus te/_bud shing de la rgyun gzhag

[\[285a\]]
[\[285a.1\]]par bya ba'i lus bzhag ste khro bo'i 'od zer gyis nyes pa drangs nas phyi rol du dor bar bsam mo/_/mchod pa'i dus su nang par snga bar khrus byas lus dris byug_/'od zer bdud rtsi lta bur bsam zhing sbyin sreg bya ste dam tshig de ni 'da' bar mi bya ba

[\[285b.5\]]gi dkyil 'khor dang phyi'i dkyil 'khor te bzhi'am nang gi 'khor yug dang mu khyud dang 'khor lo dang lha'i gnas dang mu ran dkar po'i nang dang phyi rol dang 'gro ris kyi gnas dang /_yang na rim pa brgyad dang ldan par bri'o/_/sgo ba nyid kyang phyag rgya'i gzugs su bri'o/_/bum pa kha dog ser po

[\[288b\]]
[\[288b.1\]]ni chos kyi sgo 'dod pa'i sgo nas so/_/rdo rje rnon po rno ba che/_/zhes bya ba ni zag pa med pa'i shes rab kyi sgo nas so/_/che ba chen po rgya mtsho che/_/zhes bya ba ni chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul gyis so/_/rdo rje pad+ma byang chub che/_/zhes bya ba ni 'khor ba'i nyes skyon dang bral 
[\[288b.2\]]ba'i tshul gyis so/_/sangs rgyas byang chub rang byung ba/_/zhes bya ba ni theg pa chen po kho nar nges pa'i tshul gyis so/_/rdo rje rgya chen rgya chen te/_/zhes bya ba ni don dam pa'i tshul gyis so/_/rdo rje sgyu ma rnam sbyong ba/_/zhes bya ba ni kun rdzob kyi tshul gyis so/_/rdo 
[\[288b.3\]]rje rgyu ste gnod sbyin che/_/zhes bya ba ni nyon mongs pa zad par byed pa'i rgyu'i tshul gyis so/_/rdo rje pad+ma rnam sbyong ba/_/zhes bya ba ni gzhan gyi nyes pa dag par byed pa'i tshul gyis so/_/rdo rje khro bo drag po che/_/zhes pa ni khro bo'i dkyil 'khor du snang ba'i tshul gyis so/_/
[\[288b.4\]]rdo rje dgra sdang 'joms pa'i gtso/_/zhes bya ba ni hUM mdzad du snang ba'i tshul gyis so/_/rdo rje 'jigs pa srung ba che/_/zhes bya ba ni rdo rje srung ba'i rang bzhin gyis so/_/rdo rje lcags kyu don yod byed/_/ces bya ba ni rgyal po'i rang bzhin gyis so/_/rdo rje ro langs 
[\[288b.5\]]ro langs che/_/zhes bya ba ni ma hA de bar sprul pa'i tshul gyis so/_/rdo rje srin po za ba po/_/zhes pa srin por snang ba'i tshul gyi sgo nas so/_/rdo rje gnod sbyin gnod sbyin che/_/zhes bya ba gnod sbyin du sprul pa'i sgo nas so/_/rdo rje gdon te gdon gyi mchog_/ces pa 
[\[288b.6\]]ni gdon du sprul pa'i sgo nas so/_/'jigs byed dag byed drag po che/_/zhes bya ba ni dri ma 'khru ba zag pa med pa'i sgo nas so/_/rdo rje 'jigs byed rngam byed pa/_/zhes pa ni phyi rol mu stegs 'joms pa'i sgo nas so/_/bsgrub dka' bsgrub pa legs pa ste/_/
[\[288b.7\]]zhes pa ni dga' ba spyod pa'i sgo nas so/_/rdo rje legs pa dgyes mdzad pa/_/zhes pa ni rdo rje legs pa'i rang bzhin gyis so/_/rdo rje dga' ba dga' ba che/_/zhes pa ni zag pa med pa'i dga' ba'i rang bzhin gyis so/_/dga' ba'i rdo rje dbang byed pa/_/zhes 
[\[289a\]]
[\[289a.1\]]bya ba ni bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhi'i rang bzhin gyis so/_/'od sdug pas ni sdug pa che/_/zhes pa ni gzi brjid rang bzhin gyis so/_/nam mkha' dang ni mnyam pa'o/_/zhes pa sprul pa'i rang bzhin gyis so/_/re ba thams cad rdzogs mdzad 
[\[289a.2\]]pa/_/zhes pa ni byams pa'i rang bzhin gyis so/_/rdo rje dbang bskur rin chen mchog_/rdo rje rgyal mtshan yon tan gtso/_/zhes pa rin chen dang /_rgyal mtshan gyi rang bzhin gyis so/_/rdo rje ye shes ye shes che/_/zhes pa ni ye shes lnga'i sgo nas so/_/rig pa 
[\[289a.3\]]bye ba tshogs kyi mchog_/ces bya ba lha mo rnams kyi bdag por snang ba'i sgo nas so/_/ha la ha la nag po che/_/zhes pa ni klu chen po'i dkyil 'khor du snang ba'i sgo nas so/_/dgra che zhing ni 'joms pa can/_/zhes pa ni 'jigs byed chen po'i dkyil 'khor du snang ba'i sgo nas so/_/
[\[289a.4\]]rdo rje 'dod pa 'dod pa che/_/zhes pa ni rdo rje chos su snang ba'i sgo nas so/_/rnyog ma dus ngan rnam 'joms pa/_/zhes pa ni 'chi bdag 'joms pa'i dkyil 'khor du snang ba'i sgo nas so/_/skad cig g.yo ba'i the tshom gcod/_/ces pa ni 'jig rten pa'i chos thams cad kyi the tshom 
[\[289a.5\]]gcod pa'i sgo nas so/_/glog bzhin zhal gyi ljags las 'phro/_/zhes pa ni chos myur du gsungs pa'i sgo nas so/_/rdo rje me ste ma rungs 'dul/_/zhes pa ni me ltar 'bar ba'i sgo nas so/_/drag po rab snang snang mdzad pa/_/zhes pa ni 'dod pa'i rgyal po'i sgo 
[\[289a.6\]]nas so/_/nyi ma stong gis gdungs pa'i zhal/_/zhes pa ni gzi brjid chen po'i sgo nas so/_/spyan dmar 'jigs par mdzad pa po/_/khro bo du ma'i 'od 'phro zhing /_/phyag rnams du ma mtshon cha brgya/_/du ma'i zhal te sku yang stong /_/'khyil cing brgyan 
[\[289a.7\]]yang 'khyil pa la/_/zhes pa ni khro bo'i dkyil 'khor du snang ba'i sgo nas so/_/sems kyi chos pas sku mi mnga'/_/rnam par rtog pa ma lus spangs/_/zhes pa ni chos kyi sku'o/_/ma rig 'joms pa'i tshangs pa ste/_/'dod chags zhe sdang srid mthar 
[\[289b\]]
[\[289b.1\]]mdzad/_/ces pa nyon mongs pa gsum spong ba'i sgo nas so/_/sgyu ma khyab 'jug rdo rje can/_/rtse gsum rtse gsum 'chang 'phrog ma/_/zhes pa ma hA de ba dang khyab 'jug dang brgya byin chung ma dang bcas par snang ba'i sgo nas so/_/'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug che/_/
[\[289b.2\]]zhes pa kun rig la sogs pa 'dod chags kyis 'dul ba la sogs pa'i sgo nas so/_/srid dang srid min rnam sbyong ba/_/zhes mos pa rab 'bring rnam pa gsum la snang ba'i sgo nas so/_/zhi zhing dul la dga' ba che/_/sangs rgyas sangs rgyas rab rtogs pa/_/
[\[289b.3\]]sangs rgyas bdag nyid sangs rgyas sku/_/zhes pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad bsdus pa'i sgo nas so/_/rdo rje rab skyes rdo rje sems/_/kun du bzang po cher bzang ba/_/zhes pa rdo rje sems dpa' dang kun du bzang po'i sgo nas so/_/mtshan rnams dam pas 
[\[289b.4\]]rnam brgyan pa/_/zhes pa ni mtshan bzang po sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis brgyan pa'i sgo nas so/_/khams gsum bdag nyid khyab pa po/_/zhes pa khams gsum du snang ba'i tshul gyis so/_/thams cad rdo rje bdag nyid gtsang /_/zhes pa 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 
[\[289b.5\]]'das pa thams cad dang /_rdo rje 'chang chen po nyid snang ba'i sgo nas so/_/de ltar bstod pa bstan nas phan yon ni gang gis zhes bya ba la sogs pas ston te skyes pa dang bud med gang gis so/_/'di bris zhes bya ba ni ras la sogs pa la bris pa'o/_/klog 'gyur tam 
[\[289b.6\]]zhes pa klog pa dang kha don byed pa'o/_/rtag tu de dag 'chang byed dam/_/zhes pa ni bsam pa dang sgom pa'i tshul gyis so/_/dran nas nyan par byed kyang rung /_/zhes pa ni dran pa sngon du 'gro bas nyan pa'o/_/kyang zhes bya ba ni rgyal mtshan gyi rtse mo la 'dogs pa dang /_
[\[289b.7\]]mchod pa la sogs pa yang bsdu ste de lta bu dag kyang ngo /_/dgos pa ni ci zhig yod ce na/_phyag na rdo rje 'dra bar 'gyur/_/zhes gsungs te rdo rje 'dzin pa chen por 'gyur zhes bya ba'i tha tshig go_/bcom ldan 'das kyis zhes bya ba la sogs pas dga' ba dang rjes su yi rang ba 
[\[290a\]]
[\[290a.1\]]ston to/_/de yang gsang sngags kyi sgo'i spyad pa spyod par 'dod pa rnams kyis dge ba'i rtsa ba bsngo zhing rjes su yi rang ste/_sprul pa'i tshogs lus la bsdus la sprul pa bya ba nas ci bder gnas so zhes bya ba'i don to/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis zhes 
[\[290a.2\]]bya ba ni shAkya thub pa dang /_des byin gyis brlabs pa'i phyag na rdo rje dang /_rdo rje 'dzin pa khro bo rnams kyis zhes bya ba'i don to/_/de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang zhes pa ni rtsa ba'i rgyud dang /_rgyud phyi ma dang /_phyi ma'i phyi ma'o/_/brgya byin dang tshangs pa la sogs pa lha'i 'khor 
[\[290a.3\]]rnams dang /_lha dang mi dang lha ma yin dang dri za dang gnod sbyin dang srin po la sogs pa zhes bya ba ni ming zhes bya ba yang shas che ba ste/_rdzu 'phrul la brten pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par dga' ste rjes su yi rang ngo zhes bya ba 'bras bu thob pas mngon 
[\[290a.4\]]par dga' ste/_rgyud sprul pas bshad pa la rjes su yi rang ba'o/_/ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba'i gzi brjid kyi rgyal po'i brtag pa zhes bya ba sngar bshad pa bzhin no/_/phyogs gcig pa zhes bya ba de kho na nyid bsdus pa dang /_dpal mchog dang po 
[\[290a.5\]]dang /_rdo rje rtse mo la sogs pa las le'u 'di phyogs gcig tu byas shing dum bur bkol ba las 'dir 'dus par bstan zhes bya ba'i tha tshig go_/ngan song rnam par sbyong byed pa'i/_/gzi brjid rgyal po snang brtsams pas/_/dge ba bdag gis ci bsags 
[\[290a.6\]]pa/_/'gro kun ngan song rnams sbyangs te/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid gyur cig_/ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba'i gzi brjid kyi rgyal po brtag pa snang ba'i rgyan zhes bya ba rdzogs so//_//rgya gar gyi mkhan po su bid+ya ka ra
